## Filter Blocks (persisted — survives redeploys)

Jul 23 (operator): legacy LONG/SHORT/Total Real/All columns removed from display — Honest v2 only (Real≈All since the Jul-15 fix; every decision consumes Sole/Epis). Legacy counters still persist server-side.
Funnel v2 (Jul 14): Sole = candidate would have TRADED but for this ONE filter (true marginal cost — rank by this, not Total). Epis = blocked episodes (edge-triggered per pair+dir, de-inflated of scan ticks). AllF = failed regardless of order (redundancy). Momentum-ladder filters only; engine gates show 0.
